- **principle-foundational-thinking**: Nội quy trước khi viết logic: chọn kiểu và cấu trúc dữ liệu lõi, thứ tự dựng khung trước hay tính năng trước, các actor song song chia sẻ gì. Cấu trúc dữ liệu đúng thì code sau tự rõ. ([lichihouse/claude-plugins](https://github.com/lichihouse/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: Only use when poteto-mode is active for this task. Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious. ([mallochio/pstack-ports](https://github.com/mallochio/pstack-ports/tree/HEAD/prime-agent/skills/principle-foundational-thinking) · 2 copies)
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: Settles core types, data structures, and shared state before writing logic. ([NikitaKurpas/agents](https://github.com/NikitaKurpas/agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/standards/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: Use before writing logic: choose core types and data structures, sequence scaffold before features, and check what concurrent actors share. Not for type-level detail (principle-type-system-discipline). ([PatrickFanella/patstack](https://github.com/PatrickFanella/patstack/tree/HEAD/library/skills/principle-foundational-thinking) · 2 copies)
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: 适用于写逻辑之前：选核心类型和数据结构、排定脚手架与功能的先后、想清楚并发行动者共享什么。把数据结构定对，下游代码就水到渠成。 ([Praying/pstack-zh](https://github.com/Praying/pstack-zh/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: 로직을 쓰기 전에 적용한다. 핵심 타입과 자료구조를 고르고, 뼈대 작업과 기능 작업의 순서를 정하고, 동시에 움직이는 주체들이 무엇을 공유하는지 묻는다. 자료구조를 제대로 잡으면 그 뒤 코드는 자명해진다. ([rimoir/ref-agent-plugins-ko](https://github.com/rimoir/ref-agent-plugins-ko/tree/HEAD/pstack/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: ロジックを書く前に適用する。コアとなる型とデータ構造の選択、足場と機能の作業順序の決定、並行するアクターが何を共有するかの問い。データ構造を正しくして、下流のコードが自明になるようにする。 ([seiei-sogen/agent-skills](https://github.com/seiei-sogen/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack-skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: For the Box kitchen project only. Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious. ([shouteck/omakase-box](https://github.com/shouteck/omakase-box/tree/HEAD/.devin/plugins/box-pstack/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious. Explicitly invoke this pstack entry as $principle-foundational-thinking. ([starrybamboo/agent-skills](https://github.com/starrybamboo/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/harness/codex/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking**: 写逻辑之前使用：选定核心类型和数据结构，安排脚手架与功能的顺序，问并发参与者共享什么。把数据结构定对，后面的代码就会变得显然。 ([zheyi420/Dev-Wiki](https://github.com/zheyi420/Dev-Wiki/tree/HEAD/AI/Agent/plugins/pstack/skills/principle-foundational-thinking))
- **principle-foundational-thinking-pi**: Pi adaptation: Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. Get the data structures right so downstream code becomes obvious. ([evoclock/pi-poteto-mode](https://github.com/evoclock/pi-poteto-mode/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-foundational-thinking-pi) · ★9)
- **principle-frame-follows-the-data**: Apply when setting up a figure: choose a closed box or an open L-frame from the data, take line widths, marker sizes and fonts from the preset, and keep to 3 to 5 major ticks. Override the preset only for a stated reason. ([taixuann/rstack](https://github.com/taixuann/rstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-frame-follows-the-data) · ★1)
- **principle-freeze-the-grid**: 기존 영상이나 cue에 붙은 음악의 시간 계약(beat, t0, meter, 첫 다운비트, hit 번호)을 지키고, 바꿔야 할 때는 다시 만들 범위와 비용을 먼저 밝히는 판단 원칙. ([ynginkim/op55](https://github.com/ynginkim/op55/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-freeze-the-grid))
- **principle-fresh-eyes-find-bugs**: Apply when reviewing issue bodies, scenarios, or findings. The author stops seeing their own mistakes; a reviewer with no prior context catches them. Fresh context is mandatory when a spawn tool exists, and self-review must be labeled as such. ([jirayusueb/qa-stack](https://github.com/jirayusueb/qa-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-fresh-eyes-find-bugs))
- **principle-freshness**: 解释行情时间、时效敏感信号、缺少信号或缓存不新时使用。区分数据时间与发现时间，并保留延迟和未知范围。 ([aicoder2048/pi-super-agent](https://github.com/aicoder2048/pi-super-agent/tree/HEAD/docs/modules/examples/stock-ta/skills/principle-freshness))
- **principle-frontend**: Routes frontend engineering through rendering, ownership, reuse, state, CSS, and performance decisions. Use when building or reorganizing a React/Vue/Svelte UI ("프론트엔드 구조 잡아줘"); choosing a React + Vite or Next.js shell, folder/public-API, or server/client boundary; improving component reuse or state ownership; selecting CSS Modules/Tailwind/CSS-in-JS and token structure; or setting dependency, bundle, and CSS performance strategy. Not for material visual/UX judgment or DESIGN.md — use design; public API/server contracts — use api/principle-backend; TypeScript-only work — use principle-program… ([GoBeromsu/craft-skills](https://github.com/GoBeromsu/craft-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-frontend))
- **principle-frontend-ui-engineering**: Builds and reviews user-facing UI that is accessible, responsive, and visibly designed. Use when building or styling pages and components, choosing a visual direction, managing UI state, or reviewing a UI or its accessibility. ([joseiciano/dotfiles](https://github.com/joseiciano/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/ai-clis/global-ai/output/claude/skills/principle-frontend-ui-engineering) · 2 copies)
- **principle-frontmatter**: A seven-line principle whose body starts on line 5. ([EvanBatten/pstack-for-cc](https://github.com/EvanBatten/pstack-for-cc/tree/HEAD/eval/parity/fixtures/skills/principle-frontmatter) · ★2)
- **principle-functional-composition**: Apply whenever writing or reshaping code, in any language. Build behavior from small pure functions composed into pipelines, keep effects at the edges, and write entry points as short orchestrators of reusable modules. ([ja153903/jimmy](https://github.com/ja153903/jimmy/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-functional-composition) · ★1)
- **principle-functional-core**: Apply when writing business logic, or a change that mixes decisions with I/O. Decisions live in pure functions, and a thin shell does the I/O. ([pdevito3/pdstack](https://github.com/pdevito3/pdstack/tree/HEAD/plugins/pdstack/skills/principle-functional-core))
- **principle-functional-programming**: 関数型プログラミングの考え方でコードを設計・実装・レビューするスキル。業務のモデルを型で表し、純粋関数による状態の遷移として処理を組み立て、I/O を端に寄せ、依存はカリー化で注入する。 TypeScript / JavaScript で新しい処理を設計・実装するとき、ドメインモデルや業務フローを型と関数で表したいとき、副作用と純粋なロジックの分け方や依存注入の方法に迷ったとき、既存コードをテストしやすい形に整理したいとき、関数型の観点でコードをレビューしてほしいときは必ずこのスキルを使うこと。 「関数型で書いて」「FP で」「純粋関数にして」「副作用を分けて」「DI どうする？」「ドメインモデルを設計して」といった依頼でも使う。 ([kazukit-dev/dotfiles](https://github.com/kazukit-dev/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/principle-functional-programming))
- **principle-gaps-are-not-passes**: WHEN a check was skipped, unavailable, timed out or returned no result before a verdict; NOT for choosing which checks to run; reports each gap and gives a verified, not verified or inconclusive verdict. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-gaps-are-not-passes) · ★37)
- **principle-gate-before-release**: Apply when asked whether a release ships. No verdict without the checklist: blockers closed or deferrable by name, regressions green, scenarios passing, flakes triaged, every claim cited. The verdict is one word first. ([jirayusueb/qa-stack](https://github.com/jirayusueb/qa-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-gate-before-release))
- **principle-gate-before-spend**: Apply when paying for the rest of a plan: a review's later stages, a fan-out's next wave, or a whole-file re-run. Decide with the evidence in hand and spend only what the decision needs. ([jirayusueb/egm-stack](https://github.com/jirayusueb/egm-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-gate-before-spend))
- **principle-generalize-from-rejection**: Apply when a mechanical gate — a lint, validator, blocked-command guard, or CI check — rejects your output for the same reason twice in one session. Fix the generation pattern producing the violation, not just the specific instance. ([EdbertChan/catstack](https://github.com/EdbertChan/catstack/tree/HEAD/corpus/skills/principle-generalize-from-rejection) · ★2)
- **principle-golang-respect-context**: Respect request lifetimes when implementing or reviewing Go operations that accept, propagate, or retain a context. Covers cancellation before work and through dependent calls, including context-bearing wrappers. ([ElmoJones3/opinionated](https://github.com/ElmoJones3/opinionated/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/principle-golang-respect-context) · ★1)
- **principle-grade-the-evidence**: Before you pass on any claim, give it one grade from the strength and independence of its evidence, never from venue, citation count, or repetition. ([kpeez/agent-toolbox](https://github.com/kpeez/agent-toolbox/tree/HEAD/plugins/lab/skills/principle-grade-the-evidence))
- **principle-guard-the-context**: Use when a step needs bulk reading (many files, long logs, large diffs) or hands work to another stage: send the reading to subagents, keep summaries and paths in the main thread, and hand off through files. ([fabricio21x/fbs](https://github.com/fabricio21x/fbs/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([elizaOS/eliza](https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★20k · 10k installs · 942 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([cursor/plugins](https://github.com/cursor/plugins) · ★17k · 6k installs)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([backnotprop/pstack](https://github.com/backnotprop/pstack) · ★4.5k · 10k installs)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([michael-denyer/pstack-claude](https://github.com/michael-denyer/pstack-claude) · ★4.5k · 1.1k installs)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Use when facing big command output, long files, repeated reads, or a planned fan-out. Hand bulk reading and analysis to subagents that report back short findings, and let the main thread hold only summaries and decisions. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/requie/endostack/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1.6k · 5 copies)

- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to child sessions; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([tellahq/opensession](https://github.com/tellahq/opensession/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pstack-suite/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★426)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to delegated tasks; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([Asymmetric-al/core](https://github.com/Asymmetric-al/core/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★356 · 9 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Route bulk exploration to isolated workers or files and bring back compact evidence summaries, not raw floods. Use when when reads, logs, fan-out, or generated output threatens to dominate context. ([sreichholf/dreamDroid](https://github.com/sreichholf/dreamDroid/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★117 · 3 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. Observe required inputs first; HOLD when they are missing rather than guessing. ([jeremybrasher/grokbot-skills](https://github.com/jeremybrasher/grokbot-skills/tree/HEAD/collections/pstack/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★38)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: WHEN large outputs, repeated reads or a long task threaten the context window; NOT for writing handovers, resuming work or mandatory delegation on ordinary work; reads bounded slices and searches before loading full payloads. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★37)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: pstack, typed-only. Load only when the user names this skill or a loaded pstack skill routes to it. Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([Agentifiers/srv-ops](https://github.com/Agentifiers/srv-ops/tree/HEAD/srv-orbs/agents/skills/pstack/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★9)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply before a large read, a long command output, or a subagent brief, not only when context is filling up. Route bulk to subagents, take thin reports back, and keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([mdsmithaustin/pstack](https://github.com/mdsmithaustin/pstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★9)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to spawned Pi workers; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([just-joshn/pi-customizations](https://github.com/just-joshn/pi-customizations/tree/HEAD/extensions/pi-pstack-v2/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★5)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: 当 context 即将填满时适用：大输出、长文件、重复读取、fan-out 规划。把 bulk 路由给子代理；主线程保留摘要，而非原始 payload。 ([fenfenxu/pstack-zh](https://github.com/fenfenxu/pstack-zh/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★4)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Guard the Context Window ([PickleBoxer/pstack](https://github.com/PickleBoxer/pstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★4)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Protect reasoning quality when files, histories, logs, screenshots, search results, or worker output are large. Use before bulk reads or fan-out and whenever the active context is filling with payload rather than decisions. ([caezium/skills](https://github.com/caezium/skills/tree/HEAD/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★3)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up from large outputs, long files, or repeated reads. Filter bulk evidence and isolate bounded exploration when delegation earns its overhead. ([HungryKelvin123/dstack](https://github.com/HungryKelvin123/dstack/tree/HEAD/plugins/dstack/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★3)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Guard the context window. Apply when the context fills up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads or planning a fan-out. ([erickb336/sage-stack](https://github.com/erickb336/sage-stack/tree/HEAD/plugins/sage-codex/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Bulk reading or parsing goes to `pstack-scout` when delegated; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([jisoq/pstack-mixdog](https://github.com/jisoq/pstack-mixdog/tree/HEAD/upstream/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★2)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to grunt-job and `.tmp/grunt/` scratch; keep summaries in the session, not raw payloads. Use for /principle-guard-the-context-window. ([lovrozagar/grunt](https://github.com/lovrozagar/grunt/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★2 · 20 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: 컨텍스트가 차오를 때 적용한다. 큰 출력, 긴 파일, 반복 읽기, fan-out 계획. 대량 작업은 서브에이전트에게 돌리고, 메인 스레드에는 원본 페이로드가 아니라 요약을 둔다. ([yjb94/jbstack](https://github.com/yjb94/jbstack/tree/HEAD/core/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★2)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when reading files, loading tool output or writing prompts for agents: every token in context must earn its place, so route bulk to subagents and pass paths, not content. ([cevr/dotfiles](https://github.com/cevr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: 大きな調査結果を担当へ分け、主担当には判断に必要な要約を残す。 ([dooochi8/dev-base](https://github.com/dooochi8/dev-base/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when the session is filling up: large query results, long notebooks, wide tables, repeated reads of big CSV, JSON, or Parquet previews, or fan-out plans across many segments. Route bulk to subagents, aggregate before printing, and keep summaries in the main thread instead of raw payloads. ([hidenobunagai/astack](https://github.com/hidenobunagai/astack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when large files, logs, or broad exploration threaten reasoning focus. ([jaydubya818/MissionControl](https://github.com/jaydubya818/MissionControl/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Use when large outputs, long files, repeated reads, or task decomposition threaten available context. ([Jeffreyw7/Simplicit](https://github.com/Jeffreyw7/Simplicit/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large query results, long debug logs, wide metadata reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to files and subagents; keep summaries and IDs in the main thread, not raw payloads. ([krika2810/sfstack](https://github.com/krika2810/sfstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: 大输出、长文件、重复读取、扇出规划使上下文逐渐填满时使用。把大块内容交给子智能体；主线程保留摘要，不保留原始大段内容。 ([NAMEWTA/skills-hub](https://github.com/NAMEWTA/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/materials/pstack/zh/claude-code/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context fills with large outputs or files. ([nodecode-ai/cells](https://github.com/nodecode-ai/cells/tree/HEAD/tools/nodecode-pstack/prose/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window) · ★1)
- **principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. - **principle-handle-message-uncertainty**: Preserve honest outcomes when requests, replies, acknowledgements, timeouts, or cancellation can be delayed, lost, duplicated, or reordered. ([ElmoJones3/opinionated](https://github.com/ElmoJones3/opinionated/tree/HEAD/skills/systems/principle-handle-message-uncertainty) · ★1)
- **principle-hide-representations**: WHEN a private wire, storage or framework representation crosses a module boundary; NOT for internal types that never leave their module; exposes domain concepts and keeps special-case policy at the edge. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-hide-representations) · ★37)
- **principle-hide-useful-complexity**: Apply when designing modules, interfaces, or abstractions. Hide cohesive complexity behind a small interface that reduces caller knowledge. ([lexedwards/daedalus](https://github.com/lexedwards/daedalus/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-hide-useful-complexity))
- **principle-hold-the-scope**: Use when a new request arrives, a design or plan comes back from someone else, or work is about to be called done. ([ShravanSunder/sunstack](https://github.com/ShravanSunder/sunstack/tree/HEAD/plugins/sunstack-dev/skills/principle-hold-the-scope))
- **principle-honest**: Tell the user when they are wrong. ([gmdkaio/kstack](https://github.com/gmdkaio/kstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-honest) · ★1)
- **principle-human-on-the-loop**: Work autonomously and loop the user in only where the work needs their judgment. Use whenever you run a workflow on the user's behalf. ([andrezzoid/newsroom](https://github.com/andrezzoid/newsroom/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-human-on-the-loop))
- **principle-humans-approve**: Apply before anything reaches a prospect or changes the brain's context. ([ycanerden/gluely](https://github.com/ycanerden/gluely/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-humans-approve) · ★3)
- **principle-i18n**: Internationalization (i18n) & localization (l10n) principles — locale-aware date, time, time-zone correctness (persist UTC in DB, render local), CLDR plural rules, message catalogs (ICU MessageFormat, gettext), translatable string composition (no concatenation), bidirectional & right-to-left layout. Auto-load when adding a new locale, formatting dates/times/numbers/currency for display, persisting timestamps, building message catalogs, composing user-facing strings from variables, working with right-to-left scripts, choosing between backend and frontend locale negotiation, or auditing UI-stri… ([lugassawan/swe-workbench](https://github.com/lugassawan/swe-workbench/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-i18n) · ★3)
- **principle-importance-is-obvious**: Apply when building or changing any UI. Make what matters, what happened, and what to do next obvious without explanation. ([tommyc10/tblock](https://github.com/tommyc10/tblock/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-importance-is-obvious))
- **principle-incremental-implementation**: Delivers a change in thin vertical slices, each green and committed before the next. Use when implementing a change that spans more than one file, picking up the next task from a plan, landing unfinished work behind a feature flag, or when a change feels too big for one step. ([joseiciano/dotfiles](https://github.com/joseiciano/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/ai-clis/global-ai/setup/skills-setup/skills/principle-incremental-implementation) · 2 copies)
- **principle-independent-verdict**: Only a verdict from a context that did not write the code counts — an author checks what it meant to build, not what it built, and an author-verified claim on its own diff is not enough. Apply before trusting any review, CI-green diff, self-certification, or self-verified work on a nontrivial change, including verdicts carried across rebases where the patch may have drifted. ([vraj-ai/skills](https://github.com/vraj-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/principles/principle-independent-verdict) · ★3)
- **principle-information-architecture**: How to decide a web app's navigational structure — page hierarchy, where a sub-view lives, how nav is grouped, and how users keep their bearings. The design step that creates structure, upstream of the heuristic audit that checks it. Use when adding a page, deciding tabs-vs-routes, grouping a nav menu, introducing a new section or audience, or reviewing whether a growing area still has a usable map. ([oskarc/AssetHub](https://github.com/oskarc/AssetHub/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/principle-information-architecture) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **principle-integrate-through-review**: Apply when delivering implementation work or deciding whether it is complete. Integrate changes through PRs with central checks and independent review rather than equating local verification with delivery. ([lexedwards/daedalus](https://github.com/lexedwards/daedalus/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-integrate-through-review))
- **principle-intent-and-contracts**: Expose concrete meaning and constraints in names, types, outcomes, values, effects, and documentation; distinguish naming from behavior changes. ([leesangb/agents](https://github.com/leesangb/agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/sb/skills/principle-intent-and-contracts) · ★1)
- **principle-intent-ladder-stop**: Apply when changing product behaviour or presentation, when purpose or contracts are missing, or when Open product questions is non-empty. ([hembrow-innovations/agent-core](https://github.com/hembrow-innovations/agent-core/tree/HEAD/stacks/heio-stack/skills/principle-intent-ladder-stop))
- **principle-interpret-observations**: Preserve evidence meaning when receiving observations, selecting current execution evidence, or calculating operational reports, rates, and coverage. Use for reporting correctness; does not grant authority to settle work or change recovery behavior. ([ElmoJones3/opinionated](https://github.com/ElmoJones3/opinionated/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/principle-interpret-observations) · ★1)
- **principle-invest**: 评估用户故事质量的六维度框架（独立性、可协商、有价值、可估算、小粒度、可测试），系统化审查需求质量。当需要编写用户故事、评审需求文档、拆分史诗故事时使用。提供分级检查清单和低质量故事的改进建议。 ([tikazyq/agentic-spec-forge](https://github.com/tikazyq/agentic-spec-forge/tree/HEAD/AGENTIC_SPEC_FORGE/spec_stage_skill/common_normal/principle-invest) · ★5)
- **principle-invest**: The quality bar for user stories — INVEST, vertical slicing, and the 3 Cs — plus the anti-patterns to reject. Other story-time skills apply it. Use when judging whether a story is well formed, or when a story-time skill points here. ([Team-Teddy-Development/story-time](https://github.com/Team-Teddy-Development/story-time/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-invest))
- **principle-investigate-read-only**: WHEN the ask is why, how, review, render, recall or diagnose; NOT for requests that also authorise the change; delivers findings and leaves the subject under study unchanged. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-investigate-read-only) · ★37)
- **principle-isolate-mutable-state**: Apply before writing or delegating work when actors may share files, branches, runtime resources, or other mutable state. Separate independent writers before coordinating unavoidable shared writes. ([lexedwards/daedalus](https://github.com/lexedwards/daedalus/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-isolate-mutable-state))
- **principle-isolate-test-data**: Apply to any test that reads or writes persistent state: databases, queues, files, accounts, caches, third-party sandboxes. Each test must create the data it needs, use identifiers that nobody else uses, and leave nothing that changes the result of another test. ([Jacky97s/qstack](https://github.com/Jacky97s/qstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-isolate-test-data) · ★1)
- **principle-judge-with-fresh-eyes**: WHEN a quality or acceptance verdict on finished work is due; NOT for routine self-verification while building; has a context that did not build the work give the verdict, or runs a clean review pass from the inventory and states the limitation. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-judge-with-fresh-eyes) · ★37)
- **principle-justify-the-complexity**: Use when adding or keeping a layer, abstraction, option, mechanism, or second path, or when sequencing a refactor or rewrite. ([ShravanSunder/sunstack](https://github.com/ShravanSunder/sunstack/tree/HEAD/plugins/sunstack-dev/skills/principle-justify-the-complexity))
- **principle-keep-gates-traceable**: Apply when proposing blockers, planning work, or reporting completion. Require only gates traceable to accepted requirements or demonstrated safety dependencies. ([lexedwards/daedalus](https://github.com/lexedwards/daedalus/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-keep-gates-traceable))
- **principle-keep-it-simple**: KISS, YAGNI and obvious code: decides whether a feature, option, flag, layer, dependency or abstraction should exist, presents the minimal version first, keeps what stays readable, and lands each change as if the design had planned for it. Use when proposing, designing, scoping, writing, changing or reviewing code, plans or specs. ([Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills](https://github.com/Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-keep-it-simple) · ★1)
- **principle-keep-one-source-of-truth**: Apply when you are about to create a second place that holds a fact: a config key or mapping, a cache or on-disk format, a document that describes what code or tests already say, or a field a tool will hold for you. ([kapetan-io/dispatch](https://github.com/kapetan-io/dispatch/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-keep-one-source-of-truth))
- **principle-keep-secrets-out**: WHEN writing reports, logs, issues, commits, docs, wiki pages or memory, quoting terminal output, or sending data to another system; NOT for refusing authorised credential setup or personal data the task needs; keeps credential values and unneeded personal data out. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-keep-secrets-out) · ★37)
- **principle-keep-the-core-pure**: WHEN domain logic lands in an adapter, handler, job or UI component; NOT for wiring that makes no domain decision; keeps decisions in pure functions behind thin framework wiring. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-keep-the-core-pure) · ★37)
- **principle-keep-tracking-aligned**: Apply when starting, switching, handing off, or completing tracked work. Keep authoritative workflow state, ownership, and relationships aligned with actual progress. ([lexedwards/daedalus](https://github.com/lexedwards/daedalus/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-keep-tracking-aligned))
- **principle-kiss**: 保持设计和实现的简单性，识别并消除不必要的复杂度。当需要架构设计评审、代码简化重构、接口设计优化时使用。覆盖代码、架构、接口、流程四大维度，提供简单性检查清单和过度复杂性的诊断修复方法。 ([tikazyq/agentic-spec-forge](https://github.com/tikazyq/agentic-spec-forge/tree/HEAD/AGENTIC_SPEC_FORGE/spec_stage_skill/common_normal/principle-kiss) · ★5)
- **principle-label-the-evidence**: WHEN writing findings, reports, handovers or verification summaries; NOT for deciding which checks to run; marks each claim's epistemic status, evidence level and source locator. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-label-the-evidence) · ★37)
- **principle-label-the-unknowns**: Apply when requirements, plans, test cases, or reports contain gaps, assumptions, or open questions. Every unknown must have a type, a state, an owner, and what it blocks. A bare TBD is not allowed. ([Jacky97s/qstack](https://github.com/Jacky97s/qstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-label-the-unknowns) · ★1)
- **principle-layout**: A folder and file layout that shows the design, and one order inside every file. ([Nusnus/nustack](https://github.com/Nusnus/nustack/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/principle-layout))
- **principle-laziness-protocol**: Apply when refactoring, evaluating diff size, or tempted to add abstractions, layers, or signal threading. - **principle-lead-with-the-answer**: WHEN explaining, answering a question, or reporting a result, status or incident; NOT for putting a choice to the user or compressing someone else's text; puts the answer or decision in the first sentence and the background after. ([mintuz/skills](https://github.com/mintuz/skills/tree/HEAD/src/principle/skills/principle-lead-with-the-answer) · ★37)
- **principle-lean**: Keeps changes small. Use before adding tests, CI, comments, error handling, fallbacks, retries, config options, dependencies or abstractions, and when reviewing a diff that has more of any of these than the task needed. ([gmdkaio/kstack](https://github.com/gmdkaio/kstack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-lean) · ★1)
- **principle-lessons-to-skill**: 发现自己第二次写同一指令，或注意到反复出现的纠正时应用。把规则编码成 lint、元数据标志、运行时检查或脚本，而非每次在会话中重复使用类似的指令修复重复的问题。 ([feiandre/HiSkills](https://github.com/feiandre/HiSkills/tree/HEAD/skills/common-skills/principle-skills/principle-lessons-to-skill))
- **principle-let-models-judge**: Splits LLM work from code: the model judges fuzzy input, code checks the answer's structure; prompts teach the class, never the case; a wrong model answer is fixed with prompt, more reasoning or another model, not like a code bug; evals run rarely. Use for any AI engineering work: code that calls an LLM, prompts, agents, tools, model choice, evals. ([Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills](https://github.com/Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-let-models-judge) · ★1)
- **principle-lifecycle-tasks-cancel-on-reentry**: Apply when a state machine's enter or lifecycle hook spawns a task (listener, subscription, async work) and can re-run for the same state: cancel the previous entry's task, in the library when you can. ([cevr/dotfiles](https://github.com/cevr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-lifecycle-tasks-cancel-on-reentry) · ★1)
- **principle-local-autonomy**: Apply when reversible local work can proceed without a permission pause. Keep agents autonomous inside the workspace while reserving external communication and consequential actions for the user. ([petalas/skills](https://github.com/petalas/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/principle-local-autonomy/skills/principle-local-autonomy) · ★1)
- **principle-long-term**: Changes built to last, so they are done once. ([Nusnus/nustack](https://github.com/Nusnus/nustack/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/principle-long-term))
- **principle-look-beyond-the-diff**: Apply before calling any change complete. The bugs reviewers find are usually outside the changed lines: other callers, other readers of the same data, test mocks of the old behavior, docs that state the old value, and texts that describe the same fact. Search the whole tree for them. ([Nikshay1/nystack](https://github.com/Nikshay1/nystack/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-look-beyond-the-diff))
- **principle-make-impossible-states-unrepresentable**: Apply when designing types or state, or when code branches a lot or repeats a shape assumption: model the domain so invalid combinations cannot be built (unions, state machines, branded primitives, exhaustive matches, types derived from the schema). ([cevr/dotfiles](https://github.com/cevr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/principle-make-impossible-states-unrepresentable) · ★1)
- **principle-make-it-smart**: Applies when a whats-going-on stage proposes a SMART question for the primary or asks about a missing SMART letter. Propose only what the input grounds. ([unfriction-ai/whats-going-on](https://github.com/unfriction-ai/whats-going-on/tree/HEAD/plugins/whats-going-on/skills/whats-going-on/principle-make-it-smart) · 2 copies)
- **principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs. ([elizaOS/eliza](https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/principle-make-operations-idempotent) · ★20k · 11k installs · 974 copies)
- **principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs. ([cursor/plugins](https://github.com/cursor/plugins) · ★17k · 5.4k installs)
- **principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs. ([backnotprop/pstack](https://github.com/backnotprop/pstack) · ★4.5k · 11k installs)
- **principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. Converge to the same end state regardless of partial prior runs. ([michael-denyer/pstack-claude](https://github.com/michael-denyer/pstack-claude) · ★4.5k · 1k installs)
